|
| TABLE A.1: Catalog of dense cores identified in the Herschel Gould Belt survey maps of the Perseus region
|
| "First-generation" product from the Herschel Gould Belt survey (HGBS - Andre al. 2010, A&A, 518, L102)
|
| Published in Pezzuto et al. (2020 - arXiv:2010.00006): "Physical properties of the diffuse medium and of dense cores in the Perseus star-forming region derived from the Herschel Gould Belt Survey observations"
|
| Acknowledgements: If you use this HGBS product for a publication, please refer to the above two papers (Pezzuto et al. 2020 and Andre et al. 2010).
|
| Description of columns:
|
|  (1)   Core running number.
|  (2)   Core name. The full name consists of the HGBS_J prefix followed by a tag created from the J2000 sexagesimal coordinates.
|  (3)   Core right ascension (J2000)
|  (4)   Core declination (J2000)
|  (5)   Detection significance derived by getsources (Men'shchikov et al. 2012) from the single-scale images at 70 micron. (Detection significance is 0.0 if the core is not visible in clean single scales.)
|  (6)   Peak flux density at 70 micron [Jy/beam].  
|  (7)   Error on the 70 micron peak flux density [Jy/beam].  
|  (8)   Contrast over the local background at 70 micron, defined as the ratio of the background-subtracted peak intensity to the local background intensity (Speak_070/Sbg). 
|  (9)   Peak flux density at 70 micron, measured after smoothing the data to a 36.3 arcsec beam [Jy/beam_500]. 
| (10)   Integrated flux density at 70 micron [Jy]. 
| (11)   Error on the 70 micron integrated flux density [Jy]. 
| (12)   Major FWHM diameter of the core at 70 micron [arcsec]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible.
| (13)   Minor FWHM diameter of the core at 70 micron [arcsec]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible. 
| (14)   Position angle of the core major axis at 70 micron, measured east of north [deg]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible.  
| (15)   Detection significance derived by getsources from the single-scale images at 160 micron. (Detection significance is 0.0 if the core is not visible in clean single scales.)
| (16)   Peak flux density at 160 micron [Jy/beam].  
| (17)   Error on the 160 micron peak flux density [Jy/beam].  
| (18)   Contrast over the local background at 160 micron, defined as the ratio of the background-subtracted peak intensity to the local background intensity (Speak_160/Sbg). 
| (19)   Peak flux density at 160 micron, measured after smoothing the data to a 36.3 arcsec beam [Jy/beam_500]. 
| (20)   Integrated flux density at 160 micron [Jy]. 
| (21)   Error on the 160 micron integrated flux density [Jy]. 
| (22)   Major FWHM diameter of the core at 160 micron [arcsec]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible.
| (23)   Minor FWHM diameter of the core at 160 micron [arcsec]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible.
| (24)   Position angle of the core major axis at 160 micron, measured east of north [deg]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible.  
| (25)   Detection significance derived by getsources from the single-scale images at 250 micron. (Detection significance is 0.0 if the core is not visible in clean single scales.)
| (26)   Peak flux density at 250 micron [Jy/beam].  
| (27)   Error on the 250 micron peak flux density [Jy/beam].  
| (28)   Contrast over the local background at 250 micron, defined as the ratio of the background-subtracted peak intensity to the local background intensity (Speak_250/Sbg). 
| (29)   Peak flux density at 250 micron, measured after smoothing the data to a 36.3 arcsec beam [Jy/beam_500]. 
| (30)   Integrated flux density at 250 micron [Jy]. 
| (31)   Error on the 250 micron integrated flux density [Jy]. 
| (32)   Major FWHM diameter of the core at 250 micron [arcsec]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible.
| (33)   Minor FWHM diameter of the core at 250 micron [arcsec]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible.
| (34)   Position angle of the core major axis at 250 micron, measured east of north [deg]. The special value of -1 is given when no size measurement was possible. 
| (35)   Detection significance derived by getsources from the single-scale images at 350 micron. (Detection significance is 0.0 if the core is not visible in clean single scales.)
| (36)   Peak flux density at 350 micron [Jy/beam].  
| (37)   Error on the 350 micron peak flux density [Jy/beam].  
| (38)   Contrast over the local background at 350 micron, defined as the ratio of the background-subtracted peak intensity to the local background intensity (Speak_350/Sbg). 
| (39)   Peak flux density at 350 micron, measured after smoothing the data to a 36.3 arcsec beam [Jy/beam_500]. 
| (40)   Integrated flux density at 350 micron [Jy]. 
| (41)   Error on the 350 micron integrated flux density [Jy]. 
| (42)   Major FWHM diameter of the core at 350 micron [arcsec]. 
| (43)   Minor FWHM diameter of the core at 350 micron [arcsec]. 
| (44)   Position angle of the core major axis at 350 micron, measured east of north [deg].  
| (45)   Detection significance derived by getsources from the single-scale images at 500 micron. (Detection significance is 0.0 if the core is not visible in clean single scales.)
| (46)   Peak flux density at 500 micron [Jy/beam].  
| (47)   Error on the 500 micron peak flux density [Jy/beam].  
| (48)   Contrast over the local background at 500 micron, defined as the ratio of the background-subtracted peak intensity to the local background intensity (Speak_500/Sbg). 
| (49)   Integrated flux density at 500 micron [Jy]. 
| (50)   Error on the 500 micron integrated flux density [Jy]. 
| (51)   Major FWHM diameter of the core at 500 micron [arcsec]. 
| (52)   Minor FWHM diameter of the core at 500 micron [arcsec]. 
| (53)   Position angle of the core major axis at 500 micron, measured east of north [degr]. 
| (54)   Detection significance derived by getsources (Men'shchikov et al. 2012) from single-scale decompositions of the high-resolution column density image. 
| (55)   Peak H_2 column density as estimated by getsources in the high-resolution column density image [10^21 cm^-2]. 
| (56)   Column density contrast over the local background as estimated by getsources in the high-resolution column density image.
| (57)   Peak column density measured in a 36.3 arcsec beam. 
| (58)   Local background H_2 column density as estimated by getsources in the high-resolution column density image [10^21 cm^-2]. 
| (59)   Major FWHM diameter of the core as measured by getsources in the high-resolution column density image [arcsec]. 
| (60)   Minor FWHM diameter of the core as measured by getsources in the high-resolution column density image [arcsec]. 
| (61)   Position angle of the core major axis measured east of north in the high-resolution column density image [arcsec]. 
| (62)   Number of Herschel bands in which the core is significant (Sig_lambda > 5), excluding the column density plane. 
| (63)   'CuTEx' flag: 2 if the getsources core has a counterpart detected by CuTeX (Molinari, S. et al. 2011) within 6 arcsec of its peak position, 1 if no close CuTeX counterpart was found but the peak position of a CuTeX source lies within the FWHM contour of the getsources core in the high-resolution column density map, 0 otherwise.
| (64)   Core type: starless, prestellar, or protostellar.
| (65)   Closest counterpart found in SIMBAD, if any, up to 6 arcsec from the Herschel peak position. 
| (66)   Closest Spitzer-identified YSO from the c2d survey (Young et al. 2015) within 6 arcsec of the Herschel peak position, if any. When present, the Spitzer source name has the form of SSTc2d JHHMMSSs+-DDMMSS. 
| (67)   Additional comments.
|
| Note: In Col. 65, SIMBAD acronyms have been used
|
